Hey guys, I just found this in the kitchen. The stove was left on and there was a towel on the eye that was hot. I’m not going to point fingers or get mad, I’m only going to ask all of you to please stop putting things on the stovetop that don’t belong there. I know y’all think it’s silly, but it’s an actual fire hazard. I’m not mad, I just want y’all to be safe

Feb 18, 2025 12:45:19 PM (Read by you after 8 seconds)
Claire Lupton
The point of keeping things off the stovetop is redundancy. Obviously you shouldn’t leave the stove on, but if you do on accident then it’s less likely to be a problem if you don’t put anything on the stovetop. The idea is that multiple things have to go wrong at once for there to be a problem
